supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-3--- ============================================================================
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-4--- Two objective gaps closed:
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-5---
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql:6:-- 1. The money tables (invoices, invoice_items, receipts) had NO audit
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-7---    triggers while inventory/projects/claims/etc. were fully audited.
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-8---    Every change to a financial document is now captured in audit_logs with
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-9---    old_data/new_data so the original values and authors are never lost.
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-10---
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-11--- 2. Revision reasons: invoices/quotations gain a transient `revision_reason`
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-12---    column. The app sends the reason in the same UPDATE payload; a BEFORE
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-13---    trigger stashes it into the transaction-local GUC and nulls the column so
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-14---    it never persists on the live row; the AFTER audit trigger copies it into
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-15---    audit_logs.revision_reason. Works through plain PostgREST PATCH because
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-16---    the BEFORE and AFTER triggers share the request's transaction.
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-17---
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-18--- Viewing: audit_logs SELECT is widened from "admin only" to finance-capable
supabase/migrations/20260812000008_finance_audit_triggers.sql-19--- roles (HR is head of Finance — see 20260812000007_hr_finance_access.sql).
